    case 'ai-quick-prompt': {
      sendAICoachMessage(t.dataset.p);
      return;
    }
    case 'add-custom-ai-ex': {
      const exIdx = parseInt(t.dataset.i, 10);
      if (parsedAIRoutine && parsedAIRoutine.exercises[exIdx]) {
        const item = parsedAIRoutine.exercises[exIdx];
        const created = createCustomExerciseFromAI(item.rawName, item.targetMuscle, item.equipment);
        item.id = created.id;
        item.matchedEx = created;
        item.isCustomNeeded = false;
        renderAIImportPreview();
      }
      return;
    }
    case 'removecursessionex': {
      if (!S.act || !Array.isArray(S.act.ex)) return;
      const exIdx = parseInt(t.dataset.i ?? S.act.curEx, 10);
      if (isNaN(exIdx) || exIdx < 0 || exIdx >= S.act.ex.length) return;
      if (S.act.ex.length <= 1) {
        toast('Cannot remove only remaining exercise. Discard workout instead.');
        return;
      }
      const exObj = X(S.act.ex[exIdx].id);
      const exName = exObj ? exObj.name : 'Exercise';
      S.act.ex.splice(exIdx, 1);
      if (S.act.curEx >= S.act.ex.length) {
        S.act.curEx = Math.max(0, S.act.ex.length - 1);
      }
      ls.set('act', S.act);
      save('act');
      toast(`Removed ${exName} from today's session`);
      render();
      return;
    }
    case 'resumesession': {
      if (S.act) {
        view = 'work';
        render();
      }
      return;
    }
    case 'openfreeworkout': {
      showFreeWorkoutBuilderModal();
      return;
    }
    case 'togglefreemuscle': {
      const m = t.dataset.muscle;
      if (!m) return;
      const idx = freeWorkoutMuscles.indexOf(m);
      if (idx >= 0) {
        if (freeWorkoutMuscles.length > 1) {
          freeWorkoutMuscles.splice(idx, 1);
        } else {
          toast('Select at least one muscle group');
          return;
        }
      } else {
        freeWorkoutMuscles.push(m);
      }
      showFreeWorkoutBuilderModal();
      return;
    }
    case 'togglefreeex': {
      const exId = t.dataset.id;
      if (!exId) return;
      if (freeWorkoutSelectedEx.has(exId)) {
        freeWorkoutSelectedEx.delete(exId);
      } else {
        freeWorkoutSelectedEx.add(exId);
      }
      const row = t.closest('.free-ex-row');
      if (row) {
        row.classList.toggle('selected', freeWorkoutSelectedEx.has(exId));
        const chk = row.querySelector('.free-ex-chk');
        if (chk) chk.checked = freeWorkoutSelectedEx.has(exId);
      }
      const countEl = $('#free-matches-count');
      const startBtn = $('.free-start-btn');
      const matchingCount = getFreeWorkoutExercises(freeWorkoutMuscles).length;
      if (countEl) countEl.innerHTML = `<b>${matchingCount}</b> exercises found (${freeWorkoutSelectedEx.size} selected)`;
      if (startBtn) startBtn.textContent = `⚡ Start Session (${freeWorkoutSelectedEx.size} Exercises)`;
      return;
    }
    case 'clearfreesel': {
      freeWorkoutSelectedEx.clear();
      const listEl = $('#free-ex-list');
      const matching = getFreeWorkoutExercises(freeWorkoutMuscles);
      if (listEl) listEl.innerHTML = renderFreeWorkoutExerciseRows(matching);
      const countEl = $('#free-matches-count');
      const startBtn = $('.free-start-btn');
      if (countEl) countEl.innerHTML = `<b>${matching.length}</b> exercises found (0 selected)`;
      if (startBtn) startBtn.textContent = `⚡ Start Session (0 Exercises)`;
      return;
    }
    case 'startfreesession': {
      startFreeWorkout();
      return;
    }
    case 'cancel':
      modal(`
        <div class="destructive-confirm-modal">
          <div style="font-size:36px;margin-bottom:8px">🛑</div>
          <h2 style="color:var(--rd);margin-bottom:8px">Discard Workout?</h2>
          <p class="t2 sm" style="margin-bottom:16px;line-height:1.5">
            Are you sure you want to discard this active workout? All logged sets for this session will be lost.
          </p>
          <div class="row g">
            <button class="btn s" data-a="close" style="flex:1">Keep Workout</button>
            <button class="btn s red" data-a="confirm-discard-workout" style="flex:1">Discard</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `);
      return;
    case 'confirm-discard-workout':
      S.act = null;
      ls.set('act', null);
      save('act');
      stopRest();
      closeModal();
      view = 'train';
      render();
      toast('Workout discarded');
      return;
    case 'confirm-restore-backup':
      closeModal();
      if (_pendingBackupData) {
        await applySanitizedBackup(_pendingBackupData);
        _pendingBackupData = null;
      }
      return;
    case 'hist': {
      const l = S.logs.find(l => String(l.id) === String(id));
      if (!l) return;
      modal(`
        <h2>${esc(l.name)}</h2>
        ${mmLog(l)}
        ${l.ex.map(x => `<div class="card"><b>${esc(X(x.id).name)}</b>${x.sets.map((s, n) => `<div class="t2">${n + 1}. ${Number(s.w) || 0} × ${Number(s.r) || 0}${s.rpe != null && s.rpe !== '' ? ' @ RPE ' + esc(s.rpe) : ''}</div>`).join('')}${x.note ? `<div class="sm">${esc(x.note)}</div>` : ''}</div>`).join('')}
        <div class="row" style="margin-top:16px;gap:8px">
          <button class="btn s outline" data-a="editpastlog" data-id="${esc(l.id)}" style="flex:1">✏️ Edit Log</button>
          <button class="btn s red" data-a="deletepastlog" data-id="${esc(l.id)}" style="flex:1">🗑️ Delete</button>
        </div>
      `);
      return;
    }
    case 'editpastlog': {
      const l = S.logs.find(x => String(x.id) === String(id));
      if (!l) return;
      closeModal();
      editPastWorkout(l);
      return;
    }
    case 'deletepastlog': {
      const logIdx = S.logs.findIndex(x => String(x.id) === String(id));
      if (logIdx >= 0) {
        const deleted = S.logs.splice(logIdx, 1)[0];
        _lastDeletedLog = { log: deleted, index: logIdx };
        save('logs', S.logs);
        closeModal();
        toast(`Deleted "${esc(deleted.name || 'workout')}" · <button class="chip" data-a="undodeletelog" style="background:var(--ac);color:#fff;border:none;margin-left:6px;padding:2px 8px;cursor:pointer">Undo</button>`, 6000);
        render();
      }
      return;
    }
    case 'undodeletelog': {
      if (_lastDeletedLog && _lastDeletedLog.log) {
        const { log, index } = _lastDeletedLog;
        S.logs.splice(index >= 0 ? index : S.logs.length, 0, log);
        _lastDeletedLog = null;
        save('logs', S.logs);
        toast(`Restored "${esc(log.name || 'workout')}" ✓`);
        render();
      }
      return;
    }
    case 'edit': {
      const r = S.routines.find(r => r.id === id);
      E = r ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(r)) : { id: uid(), name: '', ex: [], isNew: 1 };
      showE();
      return;
    }
    case 'adde': {
      syncEFromForm();
      P = exId => {
        const exData = X(exId);
        const isCardio = exData.type === 'timed_cardio' || exData.t === 't' || isCardioOrMobilityExercise(exData.id);
        const isBw = exData.type === 'bodyweight' || exData.t === 'b';
        E.ex.push({
          id: exData.id,
          sets: 3,
          reps: isCardio ? 30 : 8,
          reps2: isCardio ? 60 : 12,
          w: (isCardio || isBw) ? 0 : 20,
          inc: 2.5,
          prog: 'linear',
          cur: isCardio ? 30 : 8,
          stalls: 0,
          note: '',
          ss: false
        });
        showE();
      };
      showExercisePicker('Choose Exercise for Routine');
      return;
    }
    case 'mvu': {
      syncEFromForm();
      const idx = +t.dataset.i;
      if (E && E.ex && idx > 0) {
        const item = E.ex.splice(idx, 1)[0];
        E.ex.splice(idx - 1, 0, item);
        showE();
      }
      return;
    }
    case 'mvd': {
      syncEFromForm();
      const idx = +t.dataset.i;
      if (E && E.ex && idx < E.ex.length - 1) {
        const item = E.ex.splice(idx, 1)[0];
        E.ex.splice(idx + 1, 0, item);
        showE();
      }
      return;
    }
    case 'rmx': {
      syncEFromForm();
      E.ex.splice(i, 1);
      showE();
      return;
    }
    case 'savee': {
      E.name = E.name.trim() || 'Routine';
      E.ex.forEach(x => {
        x.sets = Math.max(1, parseInt(x.sets, 10) || 1);
        x.reps = Math.max(1, parseInt(x.reps, 10) || 1);
        x.reps2 = Math.max(x.reps, parseInt(x.reps2, 10) || x.reps);
        x.w = Math.max(0, parseFloat(x.w) || 0);
        x.inc = Math.max(0.25, parseFloat(x.inc) || 2.5);
        x.cur = x.prog === 'double' ? Math.max(x.reps, Math.min(x.cur || x.reps, x.reps2)) : x.reps;
      });
      const n = S.routines.findIndex(r => r.id === E.id), r = { ...E };
      delete r.isNew;
      n < 0 ? S.routines.push(r) : S.routines[n] = r;
      save('routines');
      close();
      break;
    }
    case 'delr': {
      const routineIdx = S.routines.findIndex(r => r.id === E.id);
      if (routineIdx >= 0) {
        const deleted = S.routines.splice(routineIdx, 1)[0];
        _lastDeletedRoutine = { routine: deleted, index: routineIdx };
        save('routines', S.routines);
        close();
        toast(`Routine "${esc(deleted.name)}" deleted · <button class="chip" data-a="undodeleteroutine" style="background:var(--ac);color:#fff;border:none;margin-left:6px;padding:2px 8px;cursor:pointer">Undo</button>`, 6000);
        render();
      }
      return;
    }
    case 'undodeleteroutine': {
      if (_lastDeletedRoutine && _lastDeletedRoutine.routine) {
        const { routine, index } = _lastDeletedRoutine;
        S.routines.splice(index >= 0 ? index : S.routines.length, 0, routine);
        _lastDeletedRoutine = null;
        save('routines', S.routines);
        toast(`Routine "${esc(routine.name)}" restored ✓`);
        render();
      }
      return;
    }
    case 'exp': exp(); return;
    case 'expcsv': expCSV(); return;
    case 'opensettings': showSettingsModal(); return;
    case 'wipe-step1': showWipeConfirmModal(); return;
    case 'wipe-confirm': {
      S.routines = []; S.logs = []; S.weights = []; S.act = null; S.sched = {};
      S.nutrition = { targetCals: 2100, targetProtein: 140, targetCarbs: 240, targetFat: 60, targetFiber: 35, meals: {} };
      save('routines', 'logs', 'weights', 'cfg', 'sched', 'act', 'nutrition');
      stopRest();
      close();
      toast('All data erased');
      view = 'train';
      render();
      return;
    }
    case 'openplatecalc': {
      const targetW = parseFloat(t.dataset.w) || 20;
      const name = t.dataset.name || 'Barbell';
      showPlateCalcModal(targetW, name);
      return;
    }
    case 'adjplate': {
      const targetW = Math.max(20, Math.min(500, parseFloat(t.dataset.w) || 20));
      const name = t.dataset.name || 'Barbell';
      showPlateCalcModal(targetW, name);
      return;
    }
    case 'histroutine': {
      histRoutine = t.dataset.r;
      render();
      return;
    }
    case 'clearhistfilter': {
      histRoutine = 'All';
      histQuery = '';
      render();
      return;
    }
    case 'wipe': showWipeConfirmModal(); return;
  }
  render();
});

// Debounced Search and Field Updates
let searchTimeout;
let pickSearchTimeout;
const handleFieldUpdate = t => {
  const i = t.dataset.i, f = t.dataset.f;

  if (f === 'sched') {
    const day = t.dataset.day;
    if (!S.sched) S.sched = {};
    if (t.value) {
      S.sched[day] = t.value;
    } else {
      delete S.sched[day];
    }
    save('sched');
    render();
    toast('Split schedule updated');
    return;
  }

  if (f === 'pastdate' && pastWorkout) {
    pastWorkout.date = t.value;
    return;
  }
  if (f === 'pasttime' && pastWorkout) {
    pastWorkout.time = t.value;
    return;
  }
  if (f === 'pastdur' && pastWorkout) {
    pastWorkout.dur = Number(t.value) || 45;
    return;
  }
  if (f === 'pastroutine' && pastWorkout) {
    const rid = t.value;
    if (rid === 'custom') {
      pastWorkout.routineId = 'custom';
    } else {
      const r = S.routines.find(rt => rt && rt.id === rid);
      if (r) {
        pastWorkout.routineId = r.id;
        pastWorkout.name = r.name;
        pastWorkout.ex = (r.ex || []).map(rx => ({
          id: rx.id,
          note: rx.note || '',
          uni: !!rx.uni,
          sets: Array.isArray(rx.sets) && rx.sets.length
            ? rx.sets.map(s => ({ w: Number(s.w) || 0, r: Number(s.r) || 8 }))
            : [
                { w: Number(rx.w) || 0, r: Number(rx.reps) || 8 },
                { w: Number(rx.w) || 0, r: Number(rx.reps) || 8 },
                { w: Number(rx.w) || 0, r: Number(rx.reps) || 8 }
              ]
        }));
      }
    }
    renderPastWorkoutModal();
    return;
  }
  if (f === 'pastname' && pastWorkout) {
    pastWorkout.name = t.value;
    return;
  }
  if (f === 'pastsetw' && pastWorkout) {
    const xIdx = parseInt(t.dataset.x, 10);
    const sIdx = parseInt(t.dataset.s, 10);
    if (pastWorkout.ex && pastWorkout.ex[xIdx] && pastWorkout.ex[xIdx].sets && pastWorkout.ex[xIdx].sets[sIdx]) {
      pastWorkout.ex[xIdx].sets[sIdx].w = Number(t.value) || 0;
    }
    return;
  }
  if (f === 'pastsetr' && pastWorkout) {
    const xIdx = parseInt(t.dataset.x, 10);
    const sIdx = parseInt(t.dataset.s, 10);
    if (pastWorkout.ex && pastWorkout.ex[xIdx] && pastWorkout.ex[xIdx].sets && pastWorkout.ex[xIdx].sets[sIdx]) {
      pastWorkout.ex[xIdx].sets[sIdx].r = Number(t.value) || 0;
    }
    return;
  }

  if (f === 'histsearch') {
    histQuery = t.value;
    clearTimeout(searchTimeout);
    searchTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
      render();
      const input = $('#hist-search-input');
      if (input && typeof input.focus === 'function') input.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }, 150);
    return;
  }

  if (f === 'libsearch') {
    libQuery = t.value;
    clearTimeout(searchTimeout);
    searchTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
      libPageSize = 40;
      updateLibResults();
      if (t && typeof t.focus === 'function') {
        t.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      }
    }, 200);
    return;
  }

  if (f === 'libeq') {
    libEquipment = t.value;
    libPageSize = 40;
    updateLibResults();
    return;
  }

  if (f === 'picksearch') {
    pickQuery = t.value;
    clearTimeout(pickSearchTimeout);
    pickSearchTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
      const list = $('#pick-list');
      if (list) list.innerHTML = renderPickResults();
      if (t && typeof t.focus === 'function') t.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }, 150);
    return;
  }

  if (f === 'swapsearch') {
    swapQuery = t.value;
    clearTimeout(searchTimeout);
    searchTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
      const list = $('#swap-list');
      if (list) list.innerHTML = renderSwapResults();
      if (t && typeof t.focus === 'function') t.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }, 150);
    return;
  }

  if (f === 'session-note' && S.act) {
    S.act.note = t.value;
    ls.set('act', S.act);
    save('act');
    return;
  }

  if (f && S.act && S.act.ex[i]) {
    const x = S.act.ex[i];
    if (f === 'note') {
      x.note = t.value;
      if (S.act.rid) {
        const r = S.routines.find(r => r.id === S.act.rid);
        const rx = r?.ex.find(e => String(e.id) === String(x.id));
        if (rx) rx.note = t.value;
      }
    } else if (x.sets && x.sets[t.dataset.j]) {
      const s = x.sets[t.dataset.j];
      if (f === 'rpe') {
        s.rpe = t.value;
        const num = parseFloat(t.value);
        s.rir = !isNaN(num) ? Math.max(0, Math.min(10, 10 - num)) : null;
      } else {
        s[f] = parseFloat(t.value) || 0;
      }
    }
    ls.set('act', S.act);
    save('act');
  }

  if (t.dataset.e && E) {
    const ef = t.dataset.e;
    if (ef === 'name') E.name = t.value;
    else if (E.ex[i]) {
      E.ex[i][ef] = ef === 'prog' ? t.value : parseFloat(t.value) || 0;
    }
  }
};

document.addEventListener('input', e => {
  handleFieldUpdate(e.target);
  if (e.target.id === 'morph-scrub-input') {
    updateMorphVisuals(parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0);
  }
  if (e.target.id && e.target.id.startsWith('calib-')) {
    updateCalibrationPreview();
  }
  if (e.target.id === 'ai-api-key-input' || e.target.id === 'ai-key-input') {
    handleAIKeyInput(e.target.value);
  }
  if (e.target.id === 'ai-model-input' || e.target.id === 'ai-custom-model-input') {
    const mVal = e.target.value.trim();
    const settings = (typeof AIConfig !== 'undefined' && AIConfig.getAISettings) ? AIConfig.getAISettings() : { models: {} };
    settings.models.custom = (typeof AIConfig !== 'undefined' && AIConfig.migrateModelId) ? AIConfig.migrateModelId(mVal) : mVal;
    if (typeof AIConfig !== 'undefined' && AIConfig.saveAISettings) AIConfig.saveAISettings(settings);
  }
  if (e.target.id === 'ai-baseurl-input' || e.target.id === 'ai-base-url-input') {
    const settings = (typeof AIConfig !== 'undefined' && AIConfig.getAISettings) ? AIConfig.getAISettings() : {};
    settings.baseUrl = e.target.value.trim();
    if (typeof AIConfig !== 'undefined' && AIConfig.saveAISettings) AIConfig.saveAISettings(settings);
  }
});
document.addEventListener('paste', e => {
  if (e.target && (e.target.id === 'ai-api-key-input' || e.target.id === 'ai-key-input')) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      handleAIKeyInput(e.target.value);
    }, 0);
  }
});
document.addEventListener('blur', e => handleFieldUpdate(e.target), true);
document.addEventListener('change', e => {
  const t = e.target;
  handleFieldUpdate(t);
  if (t.id === 'f' && t.files[0]) {
    imp(t.files[0]);
    t.value = '';
  }
  if (t.id === 'ai-import-file' && t.files && t.files[0]) {
    const file = t.files[0];
    const nameEl = document.querySelector('#ai-import-file-name');
    if (nameEl) nameEl.textContent = file.name;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = ev => {
      const textEl = document.querySelector('#ai-import-textarea') || document.querySelector('#ai-import-text');
      if (textEl) textEl.value = ev.target.result;
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
    return;
  }
  if (t.id === 'ai-provider-select') {
    const prov = t.value;
    const settings = (typeof AIConfig !== 'undefined' && AIConfig.getAISettings) ? AIConfig.getAISettings() : {};
    settings.provider = prov;
    if (prov !== 'auto') {
      settings.resolvedProvider = prov;
    }
    if (typeof AIConfig !== 'undefined' && AIConfig.saveAISettings) {
      AIConfig.saveAISettings(settings);
    }
    updateAIModalFields(prov);
    return;
  }
  if (t.id === 'ai-model-select' || t.id === 'ai-model-input' || t.id === 'ai-custom-model-input') {
    const mVal = t.value.trim ? t.value.trim() : t.value;
    const provSelect = document.querySelector('#ai-provider-select');
    const settings = (typeof AIConfig !== 'undefined' && AIConfig.getAISettings) ? AIConfig.getAISettings() : { models: {} };
    const curProv = provSelect ? provSelect.value : (settings.provider || 'auto');
    const effectiveProv = curProv === 'auto' ? (settings.resolvedProvider || 'groq') : curProv;
    settings.models[effectiveProv] = (typeof AIConfig !== 'undefined' && AIConfig.migrateModelId) ? AIConfig.migrateModelId(mVal) : mVal;
    if (typeof AIConfig !== 'undefined' && AIConfig.saveAISettings) {
      AIConfig.saveAISettings(settings);
    }
    return;
  }
  if (t.id === 'ai-baseurl-input' || t.id === 'ai-base-url-input') {
    const settings = (typeof AIConfig !== 'undefined' && AIConfig.getAISettings) ? AIConfig.getAISettings() : {};
    settings.baseUrl = t.value.trim();
    if (typeof AIConfig !== 'undefined' && AIConfig.saveAISettings) {
      AIConfig.saveAISettings(settings);
    }
    return;
  }
  if (t.id === 'calib-goal') {
    const gEl = $('#calib-gender');
    const targetBfEl = $('#calib-target-bf');
    if (targetBfEl) {
      const g = gEl?.value || S.cfg.bodyModel || 'male';
      if (t.value === 'sixpack') targetBfEl.value = g === 'female' ? '18.0' : '11.0';
      else if (t.value === 'bulk') targetBfEl.value = g === 'female' ? '22.0' : '14.0';
      else targetBfEl.value = g === 'female' ? '19.5' : '12.5';
    }
    updateCalibrationPreview();
  }
  if (t.id === 'calib-gender') {
    const hipField = $('#calib-hip-field');
    if (hipField) hipField.style.display = t.value === 'female' ? '' : 'none';
    const targetBfEl = $('#calib-target-bf');
    const goalEl = $('#calib-goal');
    if (targetBfEl) {
      if (goalEl?.value === 'sixpack') targetBfEl.value = t.value === 'female' ? '18.0' : '11.0';
      else if (goalEl?.value === 'bulk') targetBfEl.value = t.value === 'female' ? '22.0' : '14.0';
      else targetBfEl.value = t.value === 'female' ? '19.5' : '12.5';
    }
    updateCalibrationPreview();
  }
  if (t.dataset.c) {
    if (t.dataset.c === 'inc') {
      S.cfg.inc = Math.max(0.25, Math.min(50, parseFloat(t.value) || 2.5));
    } else if (t.dataset.c === 'bodyModel') {
      S.cfg.bodyModel = t.value === 'female' ? 'female' : 'male';
    } else if (t.dataset.c === 'dumbbellWeightMode') {
      S.cfg.dumbbellWeightMode = t.value === 'as_logged' ? 'as_logged' : 'per_hand';
    } else if (t.dataset.c === 'weekStartDay') {
      S.cfg.weekStartDay = t.value === 'sun' ? 'sun' : 'mon';
      render();
    } else {
      S.cfg[t.dataset.c] = t.type === 'checkbox' ? t.checked : Math.max(5, +t.value || 90);
    }
    save('cfg');
  }
});

// Swipe handling: horizontal swipe on active exercise media navigates exercises; swipe left on set row deletes set
let sw, heroSw;
document.addEventListener('touchstart', e => {
  unlock();
  const mediaEl = e.target.closest('.work-hero-media');
  if (mediaEl && S.act && S.act.ex.length > 1) {
    heroSw = { x: e.touches[0].clientX, y: e.touches[0].clientY };
  }
  const r = e.target.closest('.set');
  sw = r && !e.target.closest('input,button') ? { r, x: e.touches[0].clientX, d: 0 } : null;
}, { passive: true });

document.addEventListener('touchmove', e => {
  if (sw) {
    sw.d = Math.min(0, e.touches[0].clientX - sw.x);
    sw.r.style.transform = `translateX(${sw.d}px)`;
  }
}, { passive: true });

const resetSwipe = e => {
  if (heroSw && e && e.changedTouches && e.changedTouches[0]) {
    const dx = e.changedTouches[0].clientX - heroSw.x;
    const dy = e.changedTouches[0].clientY - heroSw.y;
    heroSw = null;
    if (Math.abs(dx) > 50 && Math.abs(dy) < 60) {
      if (dx < 0 && S.act && S.act.curEx < S.act.ex.length - 1) {
        S.act.curEx++;
        save('act');
        render();
        return;
      } else if (dx > 0 && S.act && S.act.curEx > 0) {
        S.act.curEx--;
        save('act');
        render();
        return;
      }
    }
  }
  heroSw = null;

  if (!sw) return;
  const { r, d } = sw;
  sw = null;
  if (d < -90) {
    const i = +r.dataset.i, j = +r.dataset.j;
    const x = S.act?.ex[i];
    if (x && x.sets && x.sets[j]) {
      const [s] = x.sets.splice(j, 1);
      save('act');
      render();
      toast('Set deleted', () => {
        x.sets.splice(j, 0, s);
        save('act');
        render();
      });
    }
  } else {
    r.style.transform = '';
  }
};

document.addEventListener('touchend', resetSwipe);
document.addEventListener('touchcancel', () => {
  if (sw) {
    sw.r.style.transform = '';
    sw = null;
  }
  heroSw = null;
});

// ---- Boot Sequence ----
async function loadExercises() {
  let raw = null;
  try {
    const res = await fetch('./exercises.json');
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Fetch failed: ' + res.status);
    raw = await res.json();
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Failed to load exercises.json:', err);
    try {
      // Fallback attempt from root path
      const res2 = await fetch('exercises.json');
      if (!res2.ok) throw new Error('Fallback fetch failed: ' + res2.status);
      raw = await res2.json();
    } catch (err2) {
      console.error('Failed to load exercises.json fallback:', err2);
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.location) {
        try {
          const path = window.location.pathname || '/';
          const dir = path.substring(0, path.lastIndexOf('/') + 1) || '/';
          const fullPath = (window.location.origin || '') + dir + 'exercises.json';
          const res3 = await fetch(fullPath);
          if (!res3.ok) throw new Error('Path-based fetch failed: ' + res3.status);
          raw = await res3.json();
        } catch (err3) {
          console.error('Failed all attempts to load exercises.json:', err3);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if (Array.isArray(raw) && raw.length > 0) {
    const normalized = raw.map(normalizeExercise);
    _global.__EXDB__ = normalized;
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') window.__EXDB__ = normalized;
    EXDB = normalized;

    const idx = (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.__EXIDX__) ? window.__EXIDX__ : (_global.__EXIDX__ || new Map());
    normalized.forEach(e => {
      if (typeof idx.set === 'function') idx.set(e.id, e);
      idx[e.id] = e;
    });
    _global.__EXIDX__ = idx;
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') window.__EXIDX__ = idx;
    EXIDX = idx;
  } else if (Array.isArray(_global.__EXDB__) && _global.__EXDB__.length > 0) {
    EXDB = _global.__EXDB__;
    EXIDX = _global.__EXIDX__;
  }
  mergeCustomExercises();
  return EXDB;
}

async function init() {
  // 1. Asynchronously load all 1,324 exercises from local exercises.json
  await loadExercises();

  // 2. Load stored state from IndexedDB / localStorage
  for (const k of ['schemaVersion', 'routines', 'logs', 'weights', 'cfg', 'sched', 'act', 'deload', 'customExercises', 'nutrition', 'photos', 'lastBackupDate']) {
    try {
      const v = await get(k);
      if (k === 'schemaVersion' && v != null) {
        S.schemaVersion = Number(v) || 1;
      } else if (k === 'cfg' && v && typeof v === 'object') {
        S.cfg = { ...S.cfg, ...v };
      } else if (k === 'deload' && v && typeof v === 'object') {
        S.deload = { ...S.deload, ...v };
      } else if (k === 'nutrition' && v && typeof v === 'object') {
        S.nutrition = {
          targetCals: 2100,
          targetProtein: 140,
          targetCarbs: 240,
          targetFat: 60,
          targetFiber: 35,
          meals: {},
          ...v
        };
      } else if (v != null) {
        S[k] = v;
      }
    } catch {
      // Storage fallback absorbs
    }
  }
  if (!S.customExercises || !Array.isArray(S.customExercises)) S.customExercises = ls.get('customExercises') || [];
  mergeCustomExercises();
  if (!S.sched || typeof S.sched !== 'object') S.sched = {};
  if (!S.deload || typeof S.deload !== 'object') S.deload = { active: false, startedAt: null, lastDeloadDate: null };
  if (!S.nutrition || typeof S.nutrition !== 'object') {
    S.nutrition = { targetCals: 2100, targetProtein: 140, targetCarbs: 240, targetFat: 60, targetFiber: 35, meals: {} };
  }

  // 2b. Execute ordered migrations on stored state
  await runMigrations(S);

  // Key hygiene: purge any lingering API keys from S.cfg and stored lift_cfg
  if (S.cfg) {
    delete S.cfg.aiKey;
    delete S.cfg.aiProvider;
    delete S.cfg.aiModel;
    delete S.cfg.apiKey;
    delete S.cfg.ai_api_key;
  }
  try {
    if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
      const storedCfg = localStorage.getItem('lift_cfg');
      if (storedCfg) {
        const parsedCfg = JSON.parse(storedCfg);
        if (parsedCfg && typeof parsedCfg === 'object') {
          let dirty = false;
          for (const k of ['aiKey', 'aiProvider', 'aiModel', 'apiKey', 'ai_api_key']) {
            if (k in parsedCfg) {
              delete parsedCfg[k];
              dirty = true;
            }
          }
          if (dirty) {
            localStorage.setItem('lift_cfg', JSON.stringify(parsedCfg));
            await put('cfg', parsedCfg);
          }
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (_) {}

  // 3. Seed starter routines & split schedule if fresh install or empty routines
  if ((await get('seeded')) == null || !S.routines || !S.routines.length) {
    const upperId = uid();
    const lowerId = uid();
    const fullBodyId = uid();

    S.routines = [
      {
        id: upperId,
        name: 'Upper Body',
        ex: [
          { id: '0025', sets: 3, reps: 8, reps2: 12, w: 20, inc: 2.5, prog: 'linear', cur: 8, stalls: 0, note: '', ss: false }, // Barbell bench press
          { id: '0047', sets: 3, reps: 8, reps2: 12, w: 20, inc: 2.5, prog: 'linear', cur: 8, stalls: 0, note: '', ss: false }, // Barbell incline bench press
          { id: '0027', sets: 3, reps: 8, reps2: 12, w: 30, inc: 2.5, prog: 'linear', cur: 8, stalls: 0, note: '', ss: false }  // Barbell bent over row
        ]
      },
      {
        id: lowerId,
        name: 'Lower Body',
        ex: [
          { id: '0043', sets: 3, reps: 5, reps2: 8, w: 20, inc: 2.5, prog: 'linear', cur: 5, stalls: 0, note: '', ss: false },  // Barbell full squat
          { id: '0032', sets: 3, reps: 5, reps2: 8, w: 40, inc: 2.5, prog: 'linear', cur: 5, stalls: 0, note: '', ss: false },  // Barbell deadlift
          { id: '0001', sets: 3, reps: 15, reps2: 20, w: 0, inc: 1, prog: 'linear', cur: 15, stalls: 0, note: '', ss: false }   // 3/4 sit-up
        ]
      },
      {
        id: fullBodyId,
        name: 'Full Body',
        ex: [
          { id: '0043', sets: 3, reps: 5, reps2: 8, w: 20, inc: 2.5, prog: 'linear', cur: 5, stalls: 0, note: '', ss: false },
          { id: '0025', sets: 3, reps: 5, reps2: 8, w: 20, inc: 2.5, prog: 'linear', cur: 5, stalls: 0, note: '', ss: false },
          { id: '0032', sets: 3, reps: 5, reps2: 8, w: 40, inc: 2.5, prog: 'linear', cur: 5, stalls: 0, note: '', ss: false }
        ]
      }
    ];

    if (!S.sched || !Object.keys(S.sched).length) {
      S.sched = {
        1: upperId,    // Monday: Upper
        3: lowerId,    // Wednesday: Lower
        5: fullBodyId  // Friday: Full Body
      };
    }

    await save('routines', 'sched');
    await put('seeded', 1);
  }

  // 4. Check active workout
  if (S.act) {
    view = 'work';
    wake();
  }

  render();
  if (!isUserOnboarded()) {
    showOnboardingModal();
  }
  navigator.storage?.persist?.();
  if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
    navigator.serviceWorker.register('./sw.js?v=7.0.3').then(reg => {
      reg.update();
    }).catch(err => console.warn('SW registration issue:', err));

    navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener('controllerchange', () => {
      if (S && S.act) {
        window._pendingSWReload = true;
        toast('Update ready. Will apply after workout.');
        return;
      }
      if (!window._swReloading) {
        window._swReloading = true;
        window.location.reload();
      }
    });
  }
}

document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  if (e.key === 'Enter' && e.target && e.target.id === 'ai-chat-input') {
    e.preventDefault();
    sendAICoachMessage();
  }
});

const C = 'mygym-core-v7.0.3';
const ASSETS = [
  './',
  './index.html',
  './style.css',
  './style.css?v=7.0.3',
  './shell.js',
  './shell.js?v=7.0.3',
  './calc.js',
  './calc.js?v=7.0.3',
  './ai-config.js',
  './ai-config.js?v=7.0.3',
  './app.js',
  './app.js?v=7.0.3',
  './vendor/three.module.js',
  './vendor/three.module.js?v=7.0.3',
  './manifest.json',
  './icon.png',
  './exercises.json',
  'exercises.json'
];
const CORE_ASSETS = Array.from(new Set(ASSETS));

self.addEventListener('install', e => {
  self.skipWaiting();
  e.waitUntil(
    caches.open(C).then(cache => cache.addAll(CORE_ASSETS))
  );
});

self.addEventListener('activate', e => {
  // Safe Offline Cache Purge: verify new cache integrity before purging old caches
  e.waitUntil(
    caches.has(C).then(hasNewCache => {
      if (!hasNewCache) return clients.claim();
      return caches.open(C).then(cache => {
        return cache.match('./index.html').then(match => {
          if (match) {
            return caches.keys().then(keys => Promise.all(
              keys.filter(k => k !== C).map(k => caches.delete(k))
            ));
          }
        });
      }).then(() => clients.claim());
    })
  );
});

self.addEventListener('fetch', e => {
  if (e.request.method !== 'GET') return;
  
  // HTML Navigation: Network-first to always fetch latest shell updates when online,
  // falling back to offline cached index.html immediately.
  if (e.request.mode === 'navigate') {
    e.respondWith(
      fetch(e.request).then(response => {
        if (response && response.status === 200) {
          const clone = response.clone();
          caches.open(C).then(cache => cache.put('./index.html', clone));
        }
        return response;
      }).catch(() => caches.match('./index.html').then(fallback => fallback || caches.match('./')))
    );
    return;
  }

  // Versioned scripts/styles & static assets: Cache first
  e.respondWith(
    caches.match(e.request).then(cached => {
      if (cached) return cached;
      
      return fetch(e.request).then(response => {
        // Cache media and valid GET requests on the fly
        if (response && response.status === 200 && (response.type === 'basic' || response.type === 'cors')) {
          const clone = response.clone();
          caches.open(C).then(cache => cache.put(e.request, clone));
        }
        return response;
      }).catch(() => {
        // Fallback to precached exercises.json if offline or alternate path
        if (e.request && e.request.url && e.request.url.includes('exercises.json')) {
          return caches.match('./exercises.json').then(fb => fb || caches.match('exercises.json'));
        }
      });
    })
  );
});

# Changelog

All notable changes to the MyGyM Progressive Web App are documented in this file.

## [7.0.3] - Onboarding Emergency Escape Hatch & Guaranteed Dismissal - 2026-11-10
- **Emergency Close Button & Escape Hatch (`app.js`)**:
  - Configured `showOnboardingModal()` with `{ hideClose: false }` to ensure the modal container close button is always rendered.
  - Added an explicit header row with `Skip / Close ✕` button (`data-a="dismiss-onboarding"`).
- **Defensive Element Resolution & Guaranteed Dismissal (`app.js`)**:
  - Wrapped `handleOnboardingSubmit()` inside a defensive `try / catch / finally` block.
  - Fallbacks added for Age, Height, Weight, Units, and Sex inputs so missing or altered DOM elements never crash execution.
  - Guaranteed modal dismissal, DOM cleanup (`m.hidden = true; m.innerHTML = ''; document.body.style.overflow = '';`), profile persistence, and toast notification inside the `finally` block.
- **Hard Boot Bypass & Runtime Gate (`app.js`)**:
  - Added synchronous runtime gate `isUserOnboarded()` checking persistent storage (`mygym_onboarded`, `mygym_shell_onboarded`, `lift_onboarded`), saved weights, workout logs, and memory state.
  - Guarded `showOnboardingModal()` and `init()` against recurring welcome modal display.

## [7.0.2] - Architectural Repair, WebGL Canvas Rendering & Macro Engine Stabilization - 2026-11-10
- **Onboarding Modal & Persistent Initialization (`app.js`)**:
  - Enforced strict onboarding check in `init()`: `const isOnboarded = S.onboarded === true || S.cfg?.onboarded === true || (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined' && localStorage.getItem('mygym_onboarded') === 'true');`.
  - Guaranteed atomic persistence of `S.onboarded = true` and `S.cfg.onboarded = true` in both memory and localStorage upon onboarding submit, completely eliminating redundant first-run prompts.
- **3D WebGL Engine & Resilient Rendering (`vendor/three.module.js`, `app.js`)**:
  - Clamped `devicePixelRatio` to `Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2)` in `WebGLRenderer.setSize` to eliminate high-DPI iOS canvas buffer distortion.
  - Wrapped `init3DViewer()` in `requestAnimationFrame(() => setTimeout(..., 60))` in `show3DAnatomyModal()` and `show3DPhysiqueModal()` to prevent black screen / 0px client dimension race conditions during modal slide-in.
  - Tuned default ambient lighting uniform (`0.55, 0.58, 0.62`) and directional light (`1.0, 0.98, 0.95`) for clear mannequin visibility.
  - Added robust pointer position extraction supporting touch events, normalized device coordinates raycasting using `canvas.getBoundingClientRect()`, and `touchAction: none`.
- **Mobile Layout & Viewport Collisions (`style.css`, `app.js`)**:
  - Elevated `.work-nav-stepper` with `z-index: 40;`, sticky bottom, and clearance padding `calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom) + 68px)` preventing occlusion by bottom navigation.
  - Enforced `.work-hero-media.collapsed { display: none !important; }` for reliable exercise demonstration GIF collapsing.
  - Added `.ai-quick-chips { flex-shrink: 0; overflow-x: auto; padding: 6px 0; margin-bottom: 8px; }` with auto-scroll to bottom on chat message append.
  - Cleaned Groq/OpenAI model IDs by stripping trailing UI annotations before network requests.
- **Scientific Calculation & Nutrition Stabilization (`calc.js`)**:
  - Refined `isSetDone` to preserve valid logged working sets (`r > 0`) where `done: true` was omitted while strictly honoring explicit `done: false` and excluding warmups.
  - Calibrated `calcMacros` with bodyweight-calibrated fat floor (`Math.max(30, Math.round(weightKg * 0.8))`) and target protein (`Math.round(weightKg * 2.0)`), eliminating extreme skew on 2,100 kcal intakes.
- **Test Suite Expansion**:
  - Added Suite 25 in `tests/test_production_issues.js`, bringing total assertions to 164 with 100% pass rate.

## [7.0.1] - First-Run Onboarding Modal Fixes & Persistence - 2026-11-10
- **Bulletproof Onboarding Action Handler**:
  - Replaced standard inputs with interactive unit (`kg`/`lbs`) and biological sex (`Male`/`Female`) pill button selectors (`.onboarding-unit-pill`, `.onboarding-sex-pill`).
  - Added dedicated button `#onboarding-submit-btn` with `data-a="submit-onboarding"` and direct `.onclick` event binding fallback.
  - Ensured atomic commit to `S.cfg`, `S.weights`, `localStorage.setItem('mygym_onboarded', 'true')`, and IndexedDB state.
- **Reload Persistence & Suppression**:
  - Guarded `init()` with `const isOnboarded = S.cfg?.onboarded || localStorage.getItem('mygym_onboarded') === 'true' || (S.weights && S.weights.length > 0);` to prevent repeated prompts on refresh.
  - Dynamically pre-filled saved age, height, and weight from existing state instead of static defaults.

## [7.0.0] - Phase 7 & 8: 3D Engine, Procedural Anatomy & Production Milestone - 2026-11-07
- **Vendored Local Three.js ES Module & Offline Precaching**:
  - Vendored local lightweight, zero-dependency Three.js ES module into `vendor/three.module.js` with full offline precaching in `sw.js`.
  - Implemented strictly on-demand lazy loading (`lazyLoadThree()`) ensuring zero first-paint delay or workout logging blocking.
- **Interactive 3D Anatomical Body Model**:
  - Engineered procedural anatomical mannequin supporting both Male (broad shoulders, taper) and Female (pelvis, waist taper) silhouettes.
  - Procedural geometries for 13 distinct muscle groups: chest, lats, traps, deltoids, biceps, triceps, forearms, abs, obliques, quads, hamstrings, glutes, and calves.
  - Full 360° touch rotation with momentum and mobile two-finger pinch-to-zoom (clamped 3.0 to 8.0 units).
  - Dynamic muscle highlighting: Amber (`#FF9F0A`) for primary targets, Cyan (`#38BDF8`) for secondary targets, and Slate (`#334155`) for neutral muscles.
  - Real-time raycasting on muscle tap opening `showMuscleDetailSheet(muscleKey)`.
- **Graceful WebGL Degradation**:
  - Robust `isWebGLAvailable()` detection; automatically falls back to 2D SVG anatomy map (`mmap`) when WebGL is absent, device is in low-power mode, or context is lost.
- **3D Physique Transformation Morph Engine**:
  - Interactive 3D mannequin deforms in real-time across V-taper, waist width, chest prominence, and abdominal definitions based on transformation progression (0% to 100%).
  - Real-time scrub slider with instant geometric morphing.
- **Pure Vanilla Animated Progress Visuals**:
  - Smooth requestAnimationFrame numeric counter (`animateCounter()`) with cubic ease-out.
  - Multi-axis weekly volume SVG radar chart (`renderVolumeRadarChart()`) charting Chest, Back, Legs, Shoulders, Arms, and Core against a 20-set weekly target.
  - Personal record celebration overlay (`triggerPRCelebration()`) with multi-color particle confetti physics burst.
  - Set-complete checkmark spring ripple animation (`.spring-ripple`) on Done buttons.
  - Strict compliance with `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` across all animations.
- **Mobile Performance Safeguards**:
  - Delta-throttled animation loop capped at 30 fps to minimize battery draw.
  - `visibilitychange` listener automatically pausing render loops when tab/app is backgrounded.
  - Explicit scene traversal and GPU resource disposal on modal close to guarantee zero memory leaks.
- **Verification, Audit & Version Milestone**:
  - 100% automated test pass rate across 159 assertion tests in `tests/test_production_issues.js`.
  - Single-source version synchronized to `7.0.0` (`mygym-core-v7.0.0`).

## [6.4.0] - Phase 5 & 6: Mobile Layout Hardening, Competitor Parity & Nutrition Engine - 2026-11-07
- **Active Workout Portrait Ergonomics (Two-Line Set Card)**:
  - Redesigned active set rows into an ergonomic two-line layout:
    * Line 1 (`.set-line-header`): Set type toggle badge (Normal, Warmup, Drop, Failure, AMRAP), previous session performance microlabel ("Prev: 60kg × 8"), and 44x44px circular thumb-friendly Done button (`.set-done-btn`).
    * Line 2 (`.set-line-inputs`): Weight stepper box (`inputmode="decimal"`) with plate calculator trigger, '×' separator, reps input (`inputmode="numeric"`), and Reps In Reserve (RIR) pill.
  - Added global single-tap text auto-selection on focus (`focusin` handler) across all set inputs for instant numerical entry.
- **Mobile Viewport & CSS Hardening**:
  - Prevented horizontal viewport blowout on narrow 320px screens with `overflow-x: hidden` on `html, body, #v, main`.
  - Added `-webkit-line-clamp: 2` on exercise names and headers to prevent multiline truncation overflow.
  - Implemented top active indicator bar for navigation tabs (`#tabs .on::after`).
  - Added body scroll locking on modal open (`document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'`) and restoration on close.
  - Enforced strict safe-area insets (`env(safe-area-inset-*)`) across top headers and bottom navigation tabs.
- **Form-Cue & Exercise Preview Media Constraints**:
  - Constrained form-cue and exercise preview media frames to `aspect-ratio: 16/9; max-height: 28vh; width: 100%; object-fit: contain;`, ensuring visual cues and set input rows remain visible without excessive vertical scrolling.
- **Local Screen Capture Tooling**:

  - Rendered Estimated 1RM, Max Weight, and Best Volume all-time record badges.
  - Implemented dual responsive SVG sparkline charts displaying Estimated 1RM Strength Trend and Session Volume Trend across recent sessions.
  - Rendered last 5 sessions timeline with set-by-set breakdown and personal notes.
- **Offline Third-Party CSV Importer (Strong, Hevy, MyGyM)**:
  - Built pure function `Calc.parseThirdPartyCSV()` supporting CSV exports from Strong, Hevy, and MyGyM with quoted multiline strings, automatic header resolution, and session grouping.
  - Added interactive CSV Import modal in Settings with live preview of parsed sessions and sets before committing to `S.logs`.

  - Added "Last Backup: X days ago" status card tracking `S.lastBackupDate` across JSON and CSV exports.
  - Integrated device storage quota meter querying `navigator.storage.estimate()` showing used vs quota MB.
- **First-Run Onboarding Flow Modal**:
  - Implemented first-run modal for fresh installations to configure units (`kg`/`lbs`), biological sex, age, height, weight, training goal (`hypertrophy`, `strength`, `fat_loss`, `maintenance`), and week start day.
  - Automatically calculates tailored daily calorie and macro targets on completion.

  - Implemented pure function `Calc.calcTrainingInsights()` detecting ACWR volume spikes (>1.5), stalled lifts across recent sessions, and neglected major muscle groups (< MEV).

## [6.3.0] - Phase 3 & 4: Security, Design System, Icons & Accessibility - 2026-11-07
- **Inline Script Purge & Strict Content Security Policy**:
  - Extracted inline self-healing and shell-reset script from `index.html` into a dedicated `shell.js` module.
  - Hardened Content-Security-Policy in `index.html`: locked `script-src` strictly to `'self'` (zero `'unsafe-inline'`), added `object-src 'none'; base-uri 'none';`.
  - Added `shell.js` to precached Service Worker assets in `sw.js` and automated version bumping in `scripts/bump-version.js`.
- **Safe HTML Tagged Template (`html` & `SafeHTML`)**:
  - Introduced `SafeHTML` class and `html` tagged template function in `app.js` with `html.raw()` support.
  - Automatically escapes untrusted strings via `esc()`, while preserving nested `SafeHTML` components, raw HTML fragments, and arrays without double-escaping.
  - Exported `SafeHTML`, `html`, `icon`, `ic`, and `IC` on `globalThis.__MYGYM__`.
- **Lucide / Phosphor Vector Icon System**:
  - Created stroke-based SVG vector icon engine with comprehensive `IC` path dictionary on 24x24 viewBox (1.75px stroke width).
  - Implemented `icon(name, opts)` generating accessible SVGs (`aria-hidden="true"` by default or `role="img" aria-label="..."` when labeled).
  - Maintained backward-compatible `ic(n, s)` helper delegating to `icon()`.
  - Replaced raw emoji icons across UI buttons, headers, rest timer, cards, and modal dialogs.
- **Copy Polish & AI Privacy Notices**:
  - Removed hype terminology across the app: changed "🪄 Ultra-Fast Biomechanical Routine Importer" to "Import Workout Routine", "Biomechanical Matching" to "Target Muscle Groups", and "MyGyM AI Coach" to "Workout Coach".
  - Added explicit, transparent privacy notices to the AI routine importer and coach drawers stating that workout data and messages are transmitted directly over HTTPS to the selected provider using the user's API key without server-side retention.
  - Enhanced markdown fence stripping (`replace(/```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```/gi, '$1')`) and brace substring extraction in `sanitizeAndValidateAIRoutine()`.
- **Design Tokens & Accessibility in CSS**:
  - Added semantic elevation tokens (`--elevation-1`, `--elevation-2`, `--elevation-3`), 4-pt spacing grid (`--sp-1` through `--sp-8`), radii, and high-contrast text tokens in `style.css`.
  - Enforced `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` across inputs, metric values, rest timers, tables, and set rows.
  - Implemented `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` disabling non-essential transitions and animations for users requesting reduced motion.
  - Split `manifest.json` icons into explicit `"any"` and `"maskable"` purposes with canonical name `"MyGyM"`.
- **Modal Accessibility & Focus Management**:
  - Added `role="dialog" aria-modal="true" tabindex="-1"` to `.sheet` modal containers.
  - Implemented `Escape` key listener to dismiss active modals, focus trapping (cycling `Tab` and `Shift+Tab` within focusable controls), and automatic focus restoration upon modal close.
  - Added `window.visualViewport` resize listener adapting sheet height when the virtual keyboard appears on mobile devices.
- **Verification & Test Suite**:
  - Added Suite 22 with 6 comprehensive assertion tests in `tests/test_production_issues.js`, bringing total passing assertions to 148/148 (100% passing).

## [Phase 2: Correctness Fixes] - 2026-11-07
- **Set Type Persistence & Canonical Normalization**: Persisted explicit `type` (`normal`, `warmup`, `drop`, `failure`, `amrap`) on every set in `finishWorkout()`. Excluded warmup sets from volume, 1RM, and PR calculations; flagged drop and failure sets so they count toward volume and fatigue without polluting standard rep PR comparisons.
- **Ordered Migration Runner (`S.schemaVersion`)**: Implemented an ordered migration framework running in `init()`:
  - Version 2: Normalized all legacy set type representations across historical logs to canonical type strings.
  - Version 3: Established `includeBodyweightInVolume: false` as the single system default and enforced unit `'kg'`.
- **Pure `calc.js` Mathematical Engine**: Purged all DOM access, Unicode emoji (`⚡`, `🔵`, `🟢`, `🟡`, `🔴`), CSS variables (`var(--...)`), and display strings from `calc.js`. Implemented machine-readable output objects with status codes (`zoneCode`); mapped UI display strings and colors exclusively within `app.js`'s view layer.
- **Anthropometry & Profile Setup State (`needs_profile`)**: Eliminated silent defaults (28yo 70kg male) in `calcBMR()`, `calcTDEE()`, and `calcMacros()`. When bio-profile metrics are unset or invalid, the engine returns explicit `{ status: 'needs_profile' }` objects, prompting the user to calibrate in the UI.
- **Exercise Kinds Classification**: Generated and embedded the `kind` field (`strength`, `bodyweight`, `assisted`, `cardio`, `mobility`, `timed`) across all 1,324 movements in `exercises.json` via `scripts/generate-exercise-kinds.js`. Updated `normalizeExercise()` to preserve `kind`.

Replaced with responsive in-app modal confirmation bottom sheets for active workout discard and app shell force updates, and non-blocking undo toasts for workout log deletion (`undodeletelog`) and routine deletion (`undodeleteroutine`).
- **Deterministic Calendar, Date & Plate Math**:
  - Immunized `getEndOfWeek()` against Daylight Saving Time (DST) hour shifts by switching from millisecond arithmetic to calendar date construction.
  - Verified start-of-week calculations for both Monday (`mon`) and Sunday (`sun`) week starts.

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Single-source version updater for MyGyM PWA
 * Updates app.js, index.html, sw.js, manifest.json, and tests
 */
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);
const rootDir = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');

const targetVersion = process.argv[2];
const shellSuffix = process.argv[3] || '';

if (!targetVersion) {
  console.error('Usage: node scripts/bump-version.js <new_version> [shell_suffix]');
  process.exit(1);
}

const shellVersion = `mygym-core-v${targetVersion}${shellSuffix ? '-' + shellSuffix : ''}`;

console.log(`Bumping MyGyM version to ${targetVersion} (Shell: ${shellVersion})...`);

// 1. app.js
const appPath = path.join(rootDir, 'app.js');
let appCode = fs.readFileSync(appPath, 'utf8');
appCode = appCode.replace(/const APP_VERSION = ['"][^'"]+['"];/, `const APP_VERSION = '${targetVersion}';`);
appCode = appCode.replace(/const SHELL_VERSION = ['"][^'"]+['"];/, `const SHELL_VERSION = '${shellVersion}';`);
appCode = appCode.replace(/navigator\.serviceWorker\.register\(['"]\.\/sw\.js\?v=[^'"]+['"]\)/, `navigator.serviceWorker.register('./sw.js?v=${targetVersion}')`);
fs.writeFileSync(appPath, appCode);
console.log('✓ Updated app.js');

// 2. index.html
const htmlPath = path.join(rootDir, 'index.html');
let htmlCode = fs.readFileSync(htmlPath, 'utf8');
htmlCode = htmlCode.replace(/href=["']\.\/style\.css\?v=[^"']+["']/, `href="./style.css?v=${targetVersion}"`);
htmlCode = htmlCode.replace(/src=["']\.\/shell\.js\?v=[^"']+["']/, `src="./shell.js?v=${targetVersion}"`);
htmlCode = htmlCode.replace(/src=["']\.\/calc\.js\?v=[^"']+["']/, `src="./calc.js?v=${targetVersion}"`);
htmlCode = htmlCode.replace(/src=["']\.\/ai-config\.js\?v=[^"']+["']/, `src="./ai-config.js?v=${targetVersion}"`);
htmlCode = htmlCode.replace(/src=["']\.\/app\.js\?v=[^"']+["']/, `src="./app.js?v=${targetVersion}"`);
fs.writeFileSync(htmlPath, htmlCode);
console.log('✓ Updated index.html');

// 2b. shell.js
const shellPath = path.join(rootDir, 'shell.js');
if (fs.existsSync(shellPath)) {
  let shellCode = fs.readFileSync(shellPath, 'utf8');
  shellCode = shellCode.replace(/var SHELL_VERSION = ['"][^'"]+['"];/, `var SHELL_VERSION = '${shellVersion}';`);
  fs.writeFileSync(shellPath, shellCode);
  console.log('✓ Updated shell.js');
}

// 3. sw.js
const swPath = path.join(rootDir, 'sw.js');
let swCode = fs.readFileSync(swPath, 'utf8');
swCode = swCode.replace(/const C = ['"][^'"]+['"];/, `const C = '${shellVersion}';`);
swCode = swCode.replace(/['"]\.\/style\.css\?v=[^'"]+['"]/, `'./style.css?v=${targetVersion}'`);
swCode = swCode.replace(/['"]\.\/shell\.js\?v=[^'"]+['"]/, `'./shell.js?v=${targetVersion}'`);
swCode = swCode.replace(/['"]\.\/calc\.js\?v=[^"']+['"]/, `'./calc.js?v=${targetVersion}'`);
swCode = swCode.replace(/['"]\.\/ai-config\.js\?v=[^"']+['"]/, `'./ai-config.js?v=${targetVersion}'`);
swCode = swCode.replace(/['"]\.\/app\.js\?v=[^"']+['"]/, `'./app.js?v=${targetVersion}'`);
swCode = swCode.replace(/['"]\.\/vendor\/three\.module\.js\?v=[^'"]+['"]/, `'./vendor/three.module.js?v=${targetVersion}'`);
fs.writeFileSync(swPath, swCode);
console.log('✓ Updated sw.js');

// 4. manifest.json
const manifestPath = path.join(rootDir, 'manifest.json');
let manifest = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(manifestPath, 'utf8'));
manifest.version = targetVersion;
fs.writeFileSync(manifestPath, JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2) + '\n');
console.log('✓ Updated manifest.json');

// 5. tests/test_production_issues.js
const testPath = path.join(rootDir, 'tests', 'test_production_issues.js');
if (fs.existsSync(testPath)) {
  let testCode = fs.readFileSync(testPath, 'utf8');
  testCode = testCode.replace(/sw\.js cache key is bumped to [^,]+,/, `sw.js cache key is bumped to ${shellVersion},`);
  testCode = testCode.replace(/const C = ['"][^'"]+['"];/, `const C = '${shellVersion}';`);
  testCode = testCode.replace(/sw\.js must use [^ ]+ cache name/, `sw.js must use ${shellVersion} cache name`);
  testCode = testCode.replace(/style\.css\?v=[^'"]+/g, `style.css?v=${targetVersion}`);
  testCode = testCode.replace(/shell\.js\?v=[^'"]+/g, `shell.js?v=${targetVersion}`);
  testCode = testCode.replace(/app\.js\?v=[^'"]+/g, `app.js?v=${targetVersion}`);
  testCode = testCode.replace(/calc\.js\?v=[^'"]+/g, `calc.js?v=${targetVersion}`);
  testCode = testCode.replace(/SHELL_VERSION = ['"][^'"]+['"]/, `SHELL_VERSION = '${shellVersion}'`);
  testCode = testCode.replace(/index\.html must use [^'\)]+/, `index.html must use ${shellVersion}`);
  testCode = testCode.replace(/assert\.equal\(api\.APP_VERSION, ['"][^'"]+['"]\);/, `assert.equal(api.APP_VERSION, '${targetVersion}');`);
  testCode = testCode.replace(/assert\.equal\(api\.SHELL_VERSION, ['"][^'"]+['"]\);/, `assert.equal(api.SHELL_VERSION, '${shellVersion}');`);
  fs.writeFileSync(testPath, testCode);
  console.log('✓ Updated tests/test_production_issues.js assertions');
}

console.log(`\nSuccessfully bumped version everywhere to ${targetVersion} (${shellVersion})!`);

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * scripts/generate-exercise-kinds.js
 * Generates the 'kind' field on every entry in exercises.json.
 * Kinds: 'strength' | 'bodyweight' | 'assisted' | 'cardio' | 'mobility' | 'timed'
 */

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);
const jsonPath = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', 'exercises.json');

const raw = fs.readFileSync(jsonPath, 'utf8');
const exercises = JSON.parse(raw);

function determineKind(e) {
  const n = String(e.n || e.name || '').toLowerCase();
  const bp = String(e.bp || '').toLowerCase();
  const eq = String(e.eq || '').toLowerCase();
  const tg = String(e.tg || '').toLowerCase();

  // 1. Assisted
  if (eq.includes('assisted') || n.includes('assisted')) {
    return 'assisted';
  }

  // 2. Mobility & Flexibility
  if (
    n.includes('stretch') ||
    n.includes('mobility') ||
    n.includes('foam roll') ||
    n.includes('yoga') ||
    n.includes('warm-up') ||
    bp === 'mobility'
  ) {
    return 'mobility';
  }

  // 3. Cardio
  if (
    bp === 'cardio' ||
    tg === 'cardiovascular system' ||
    n.includes('treadmill') ||
    n.includes('elliptical') ||
    n.includes('cycling') ||
    n.includes('stationary bike') ||
    n.includes('rowing machine') ||
    n.includes('jump rope') ||
    n.includes('jumping rope') ||
    n.includes('jumping jack') ||
    n.includes('burpee') ||
    n.includes('high knee') ||
    n.includes('running') ||
    n.includes('jogging')
  ) {
    return 'cardio';
  }

  // 4. Timed (Isometric holds)
  if (
    n.includes('plank') ||
    n.includes('wall sit') ||
    n.includes('hollow hold') ||
    n.includes('l-sit') ||
    n.includes('dead hang')
  ) {
    return 'timed';
  }

  // 5. Bodyweight
  if (
    eq === 'body weight' ||
    eq === 'none' ||
    eq === 'bodyweight' ||
    n.includes('bodyweight') ||
    e.t === 'b'
  ) {
    return 'bodyweight';
  }

  // 6. Strength (default for resistance movements)
  return 'strength';
}

let counts = {
  strength: 0,
  bodyweight: 0,
  assisted: 0,
  cardio: 0,
  mobility: 0,
  timed: 0
};

const updated = exercises.map(e => {
  const kind = determineKind(e);
  counts[kind]++;
  return {
    ...e,
    kind
  };
});

fs.writeFileSync(jsonPath, JSON.stringify(updated, null, 2) + '\n', 'utf8');

console.log(`Updated ${updated.length} exercises with 'kind' field in exercises.json:`);
console.table(counts);

================================================================================
<<< END FILE: scripts/generate-exercise-kinds.js

/**
 * scripts/capture-screens.js
 * Captures responsive view screenshots across 320px, 390px, and 768px viewports.
 */
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const rootDir = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
const outDir = path.join(rootDir, 'screenshots');

const viewports = [
  { name: 'mobile-small-320', width: 320, height: 640 },
  { name: 'mobile-iphone-390', width: 390, height: 844 },
  { name: 'tablet-ipad-768', width: 768, height: 1024 }
];

const views = [
  { tab: 'train', name: '01-train' },
  { tab: 'active', name: '02-active-workout' },
  { tab: 'lib', name: '03-library' },
  { tab: 'cal', name: '04-calendar' },
  { tab: 'body', name: '05-body-nutrition' }
];

async function capture() {
  if (!fs.existsSync(outDir)) {
    fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });
  }

  let playwright;
  try {
    playwright = await import('playwright');
  } catch {
    console.log('Playwright is not installed in local environment.');
    console.log('To run screen captures: npx playwright test or npm i -D playwright && node scripts/capture-screens.js');
    console.log(`Configured captures for viewports: ${viewports.map(v => v.width + 'px').join(', ')}`);
    console.log(`Configured capture targets for views: ${views.map(v => v.name).join(', ')}`);
    return;
  }

  const browser = await playwright.chromium.launch();
  const context = await browser.newContext();
  const page = await context.newPage();

  const fileUrl = 'file://' + path.join(rootDir, 'index.html');
  await page.goto(fileUrl);

  for (const vp of viewports) {
    await page.setViewportSize({ width: vp.width, height: vp.height });
    for (const v of views) {
      if (v.tab === 'active') {
        await page.evaluate(() => {
          if (window.__MYGYM__ && window.__MYGYM__.S) {
            window.__MYGYM__.S.act = {
              id: 'sample_workout',
              name: 'Push Hypertrophy',
              t0: Date.now() - 1800000,
              curEx: 0,
              ex: [{ id: '0025', sets: [{ w: 80, r: 8, done: true }, { w: 80, r: 8, done: false }] }]
            };
            window.__MYGYM__.render();
          }
        });
      } else {
        await page.evaluate((tabKey) => {
          const tabBtn = document.querySelector(`[data-k="${tabKey}"]`);
          if (tabBtn) tabBtn.click();
        }, v.tab);
      }
      await page.waitForTimeout(200);
      const outPath = path.join(outDir, `${v.name}-${vp.name}.png`);
      await page.screenshot({ path: outPath, fullPage: false });
      console.log(`✓ Captured ${outPath}`);
    }
  }

  await browser.close();
  console.log('Finished capturing all screen viewports.');
}

capture().catch(err => {
  console.error('Screen capture error:', err.message);
  process.exit(1);
});

================================================================================
<<< END FILE: scripts/capture-screens.js

>>> START FILE: scripts/consolidate.js

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);
const rootDir = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');

const manifestPath = path.join(rootDir, 'manifest.json');
let appVersion = '7.0.0';
if (fs.existsSync(manifestPath)) {
  try {
    const manifest = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(manifestPath, 'utf8'));
    if (manifest.version) appVersion = manifest.version;
  } catch (_) {}
}

const files = [
  'manifest.json',
  'index.html',
  'shell.js',
  'style.css',
  'calc.js',
  'ai-config.js',
  'vendor/three.module.js',
  'app.js',
  'sw.js',
  'CHANGELOG.md',
  'scripts/bump-version.js',
  'scripts/generate-exercise-kinds.js',
  'scripts/capture-screens.js',
  'scripts/consolidate.js',
  'tests/test_production_issues.js'
];

let toc = 'TABLE OF CONTENTS:\n';
const fileContents = [];

files.forEach((relPath, idx) => {
  const fullPath = path.join(rootDir, relPath);
  if (fs.existsSync(fullPath)) {
    const stats = fs.statSync(fullPath);
    const kb = (stats.size / 1024).toFixed(1);
    toc += `${idx + 1}. ${relPath} (${kb} KB)\n`;
    fileContents.push({
      relPath,
      content: fs.readFileSync(fullPath, 'utf8')
    });
  }
});

const header = `================================================================================
MYGYM PWA - COMPLETE CONSOLIDATED SOURCE CODEBASE (v${appVersion})
Generated at: ${new Date().toISOString()}
Architecture: Zero-Backend Offline-First Progressive Web App
================================================================================

${toc}================================================================================
`;

let output = header;
for (const file of fileContents) {
  output += `\n================================================================================\n`;
  output += `>>> START FILE: ${file.relPath}\n`;
  output += `================================================================================\n`;
  output += file.content;
  if (!file.content.endsWith('\n')) output += '\n';
  output += `================================================================================\n`;
  output += `<<< END FILE: ${file.relPath}\n`;
  output += `================================================================================\n`;
}

const targetPath = path.join(rootDir, 'MYGYM_CONSOLIDATED_CODEBASE.txt');
fs.writeFileSync(targetPath, output, 'utf8');
console.log(`✓ Updated ${targetPath} (${(fs.statSync(targetPath).size / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB)`);

// Mock DOM Environment
class MockElement {
  constructor(tag = 'div', className = '', id = '') {
    this.tagName = tag.toUpperCase();
    this.className = className;
    this.id = id;
    this._innerHTML = '';
    this.style = {};
    this.children = [];
    this.parentElement = null;
    this.dataset = {};
    this.scrollLeft = 0;
    this.scrollTop = 0;
    this.hidden = false;
    this._lastScrollIntoView = null;
    this.classList = {
      _classes: new Set(className ? className.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean) : []),
      add: (c) => this.classList._classes.add(c),
      remove: (c) => this.classList._classes.delete(c),
      toggle: (c, force) => {
        if (force === undefined) {
          if (this.classList._classes.has(c)) this.classList._classes.delete(c);
          else this.classList._classes.add(c);
        } else if (force) {
          this.classList._classes.add(c);
        } else {
          this.classList._classes.delete(c);
        }
      },
      contains: (c) => this.classList._classes.has(c)
    };
  }

  get textContent() {
    return this._textContent != null ? this._textContent : this._innerHTML.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '');
  }

  set textContent(val) {
    this._textContent = String(val);
    this._innerHTML = String(val);
  }

  get innerHTML() {
    return this._innerHTML;
  }

  set innerHTML(val) {
    this._innerHTML = String(val);
    this._syncChildrenFromHtml();
  }

  get outerHTML() {
    return `<${this.tagName.toLowerCase()} class="${this.className}" id="${this.id}">${this._innerHTML}</${this.tagName.toLowerCase()}>`;
  }

  set outerHTML(val) {
    if (this.parentElement) {
      const temp = new MockElement('div');
      temp.innerHTML = val;
      const newChild = temp.children[0] || temp;
      const idx = this.parentElement.children.indexOf(this);
      if (idx !== -1) {
        newChild.parentElement = this.parentElement;
        this.parentElement.children[idx] = newChild;
      }
    } else {
      this.innerHTML = val;
    }
  }

  _syncChildrenFromHtml() {
    // Basic parser for nested mockup elements
    if (this.className.includes('work-stepper-pills') || this._innerHTML.includes('work-step-pill')) {
      const matches = this._innerHTML.match(/<button[^>]*class="([^"]*work-step-pill[^"]*)"[^>]*data-i="(\d+)"[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/button>/g) || [];
      this.children = matches.map((m, idx) => {
        const p = new MockElement('button');
        const cMatch = m.match(/class="([^"]*)"/);
        p.className = cMatch ? cMatch[1] : 'work-step-pill';
        p.dataset.i = String(idx);
        p.parentElement = this;
        return p;
      });
    }
  }

  querySelector(sel) {
    if (sel.startsWith('#')) {
      const id = sel.slice(1);
      if (this.id === id) return this;
      for (const c of this.children) {
        const found = c.querySelector(sel);
        if (found) return found;
      }
      return null;
    }
    if (sel.startsWith('.')) {
      const cls = sel.slice(1).split('.')[0];
      if (this.className.split(/\s+/).includes(cls)) {
        if (sel === '.work-step-pill.active') {
          return this.className.includes('active') ? this : null;
        }
        return this;
      }
      for (const c of this.children) {
        const found = c.querySelector(sel);
        if (found) return found;
      }
      return null;
    }
    return null;
  }

  querySelectorAll(sel) {
    const results = [];
    const check = (el) => {
      if (sel.startsWith('.')) {
        const cls = sel.slice(1).split('.')[0];
        if (el.className.split(/\s+/).includes(cls)) {
          results.push(el);
        }
      }
      for (const c of el.children) check(c);
    };
    check(this);
    return results;
  }

  scrollIntoView(opts) {
    this._lastScrollIntoView = opts;
  }

  insertAdjacentHTML(pos, html) {
    const temp = new MockElement('div');
    temp.innerHTML = html;
    if (pos === 'beforeend') {
      temp.children.forEach(c => {
        c.parentElement = this;
        this.children.push(c);
      });
      this._innerHTML += html;
    }
  }

  remove() {
    if (this.parentElement) {
      this.parentElement.children = this.parentElement.children.filter(c => c !== this);
    }
  }

  appendChild(child) {
    if (child) {
      child.parentElement = this;
      this.children.push(child);
    }
    return child;
  }

  addEventListener() {}
  removeEventListener() {}
}

// TEST SUITE 1: COLD-START BASELINE GATE (ACWR & Deload Protection)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
console.log('Suite 1: ACWR Cold-Start Baseline Gate & Deload Protection');

it('Cold-start (single workout today): ACWR 4.00 spike suppressed & status calibrated', () => {
  const now = Date.now();
  // 1 workout logged today: 3 sets of 100 kg x 10 reps = 3000 kg
  const singleLog = [
    {
      id: 'log1',
      date: now,
      ex: [
        {
          id: '0025',
          sets: [
            { w: 100, r: 10, done: true },
            { w: 100, r: 10, done: true },
            { w: 100, r: 10, done: true }
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  ];

  const w = api.calcWorkload(singleLog);
  assert.equal(w.isCalibrating, true, 'isCalibrating must be true for cold start');
  assert.equal(w.acwr, 'Calibrating', 'acwr must be "Calibrating" instead of 4.00');
  assert.equal(w.acwrDisplay, 'Calibrating', 'acwrDisplay must display "Calibrating"');
  assert.equal(w.acwrNum, null, 'acwrNum must be null during calibration');
  assert(w.status.includes('⚡ Calibrating Baseline (Day 1 of 14) 🔵'), `Status must display Day 1 of 14 badge: ${w.status}`);
  assert.equal(w.tooltip, 'Log workouts over 2-3 weeks to build your chronic fatigue baseline.');

  // Hard block deload card
  const deload = api.checkDeloadStatus(singleLog);
  assert.equal(deload.recommended, false, 'checkDeloadStatus MUST return recommended: false during calibration');
});

it('Baseline gate: < 7 distinct workout days gates ACWR even after 10 days', () => {
  const now = Date.now();
  const dayMs = 86400000;
  // 4 distinct workout days spread across trailing 10 days
  const logs = [
    { id: 'l1', date: now - 9 * dayMs, ex: [{ id: '0025', sets: [{ w: 80, r: 10, done: true }] }] },
    { id: 'l2', date: now - 6 * dayMs, ex: [{ id: '0025', sets: [{ w: 80, r: 10, done: true }] }] },
    { id: 'l3', date: now - 3 * dayMs, ex: [{ id: '0025', sets: [{ w: 80, r: 10, done: true }] }] },
    { id: 'l4', date: now, ex: [{ id: '0025', sets: [{ w: 80, r: 10, done: true }] }] }
  ];

  const w = api.calcWorkload(logs);
  assert.equal(w.distinctWorkoutDays, 4);
  assert.equal(w.isCalibrating, true, 'isCalibrating must be true when distinctWorkoutDays < 7');
  assert.equal(w.acwr, 'Calibrating');
  assert(w.status.includes('⚡ Calibrating Baseline (Day 10 of 14) 🔵'));

  const deload = api.checkDeloadStatus(logs);
  assert.equal(deload.recommended, false, 'Deload card must remain hard-blocked');
});

it('Baseline gate: 7 distinct days but < 14 days history gates ACWR', () => {
  const now = Date.now();
  const dayMs = 86400000;
  // 7 distinct days in 7 consecutive calendar days (totalHistoryDays = 7)
  const logs = [];
  for (let d = 0; d < 7; d++) {
    logs.push({
      id: `l_${d}`,
      date: now - d * dayMs,
      ex: [{ id: '0025', sets: [{ w: 50, r: 10, done: true }] }]
    });
  }

  const w = api.calcWorkload(logs);
  assert.equal(w.distinctWorkoutDays, 7);
  assert.equal(w.isCalibrating, true, 'isCalibrating must be true when totalHistoryDays < 14');
  assert.equal(w.acwr, 'Calibrating');

  const deload = api.checkDeloadStatus(logs);
  assert.equal(deload.recommended, false, 'Deload must remain hard-blocked during baseline calibration');
});

it('Calibrated user (>= 7 distinct days AND >= 14 days history): Normal ACWR calculated', () => {
  const now = Date.now();
  const dayMs = 86400000;
  // 8 workouts spread across 21 days
  const logs = [
    { id: 'l1', date: now - 20 * dayMs, ex: [{ id: '0025', sets: [{ w: 80, r: 10, done: true }] }] },
    { id: 'l2', date: now - 17 * dayMs, ex: [{ id: '0025', sets: [{ w: 80, r: 10, done: true }] }] },
    { id: 'l3', date: now - 14 * dayMs, ex: [{ id: '0025', sets: [{ w: 80, r: 10, done: true }] }] },
    { id: 'l4', date: now - 11 * dayMs, ex: [{ id: '0025', sets: [{ w: 80, r: 10, done: true }] }] },
    { id: 'l5', date: now - 8 * dayMs, ex: [{ id: '0025', sets: [{ w: 80, r: 10, done: true }] }] },
    { id: 'l6', date: now - 5 * dayMs, ex: [{ id: '0025', sets: [{ w: 80, r: 10, done: true }] }] },
    { id: 'l7', date: now - 2 * dayMs, ex: [{ id: '0025', sets: [{ w: 80, r: 10, done: true }] }] },
    { id: 'l8', date: now, ex: [{ id: '0025', sets: [{ w: 80, r: 10, done: true }] }] }
  ];

  const w = api.calcWorkload(logs);
  assert.equal(w.isCalibrating, false, 'isCalibrating must be false once baseline is established');
  assert.equal(typeof w.acwr, 'number', 'ACWR must be a numeric value');
  assert(w.acwr > 0);
  assert.notEqual(w.status, 'Calibrating');
});

console.log('\nSuite 2: Dual-Exercise Superset Pairing Flow');

it('getSupersetInfo pairs linked exercises correctly into A1/A2 block', () => {
  const act = {
    curEx: 0,
    ex: [
      { id: '0025', ss: true, ssRole: 'A', ssPair: 'ss_0', sets: [{ done: false }, { done: false }] },
      { id: '0027', ss: true, ssRole: 'B', ssPair: 'ss_0', sets: [{ done: false }, { done: false }] }
    ]
  };

  const infoA = api.getSupersetInfo(act, 0);
  assert.equal(infoA.isSuperset, true);
  assert.equal(infoA.isPaired, true);
  assert.equal(infoA.role, 'A');
  assert.equal(infoA.exAIndex, 0);
  assert.equal(infoA.exBIndex, 1);
  assert.equal(infoA.round, 1);
  assert.equal(infoA.totalRounds, 2);

  const infoB = api.getSupersetInfo(act, 1);
  assert.equal(infoB.isSuperset, true);
  assert.equal(infoB.isPaired, true);
  assert.equal(infoB.role, 'B');
  assert.equal(infoB.exAIndex, 0);
  assert.equal(infoB.exBIndex, 1);
});

it('showSupersetPicker renders dynamic modal sheet with current session and library options', () => {
  api.S.act = {
    name: 'Full Body',
    curEx: 0,
    ex: [
      { id: '0025', ss: false, sets: [{ done: false }] }, // Bench Press
      { id: '0047', ss: false, sets: [{ done: false }] }, // Incline Bench
      { id: '0027', ss: false, sets: [{ done: false }] }  // Row
    ]
  };

  elementsMap['#m'].innerHTML = '';
  elementsMap['#m'].hidden = true;

  api.showSupersetPicker(0);

  const html = elementsMap['#m'].innerHTML;
  assert.equal(elementsMap['#m'].hidden, false, 'Modal must open');
  assert(html.includes('ss-picker-sheet'), 'Must render ss-picker-sheet');
  assert(html.includes('1. Choose from Current Session'), 'Must have Section 1');
  assert(html.includes('2. Add & Pair from Library'), 'Must have Section 2');
  assert(html.includes('ss-modal-close-btn'), 'Must have circular frosted close button');
  assert(html.includes('ss-pick-item'), 'Must have ss-pick-item flex row');
  assert(html.includes('ss-link-pill-btn'), 'Must have ss-link-pill-btn');
  assert(html.includes('ss-source-badge'), 'Must have source exercise badge');
  assert(html.includes('Need an exercise not in today\'s split?'), 'Must have library helper text');
  assert(html.includes('+ Browse Exercise Library'), 'Must have browse library button text');
  assert(html.includes('data-a="browseandpair"'), 'Must have browse library button');
  assert(html.includes('data-a="pairwithex"'), 'Must have pairwithex action');
  assert(!html.includes('data-target="0"'), 'Section 1 must exclude current exercise itself');
  assert(html.includes('data-target="1"'), 'Section 1 must include exercise 1');
  assert(html.includes('data-target="2"'), 'Section 1 must include exercise 2');

  // Verify empty state when all exercises in session are paired
  api.S.act.ex[1].ss = true;
  api.S.act.ex[1].ssGroup = 'ss_other';
  api.S.act.ex[2].ss = true;
  api.S.act.ex[2].ssGroup = 'ss_other';
  api.showSupersetPicker(0);
  const emptyHtml = elementsMap['#m'].innerHTML;
  assert(emptyHtml.includes('All session exercises are currently paired.'), 'Must show clean empty state message');
});

it('pairExercises reorders non-adjacent exercises to create an immediate adjacent A1 + A2 block', () => {
  // Initial workout order: [0025 (Bench), 0047 (Incline), 0043 (Squat), 0027 (Row)]
  api.S.act = {
    name: 'Workout',
    curEx: 0,
    ex: [
      { id: '0025', sets: [{ done: false }, { done: false }] }, // Bench (idx 0)
      { id: '0047', sets: [{ done: false }, { done: false }] }, // Incline (idx 1)
      { id: '0043', sets: [{ done: false }, { done: false }] }, // Squat (idx 2)
      { id: '0027', sets: [{ done: false }, { done: false }] }  // Row (idx 3)
    ]
  };

  // Pair Bench (index 0) with non-adjacent Row (index 3)
  api.pairExercises(0, 3);

  // Assertions for reordering and cohesive blocking
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex.length, 4, 'Total exercises count remains 4');
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[0].id, '0025', 'Exercise A1 remains Bench Press at index 0');
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[1].id, '0027', 'Exercise A2 is now Row reordered adjacent to index 1');
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[2].id, '0047', 'Incline shifted to index 2');
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[3].id, '0043', 'Squat shifted to index 3');

  // Verify superset attributes
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[0].ss, true);
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[0].ssRole, 'A');
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[1].ss, true);
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[1].ssRole, 'B');
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[0].ssGroup, api.S.act.ex[1].ssGroup, 'Matching ssGroup');

  // Verify getSupersetInfo recognizes adjacent pair
  const ss = api.getSupersetInfo(api.S.act, 0);
  assert.equal(ss.isSuperset, true);
  assert.equal(ss.isPaired, true);
  assert.equal(ss.exAIndex, 0);
  assert.equal(ss.exBIndex, 1);
});

it('addAndPairFromLibrary dynamically appends from library directly next to current exercise', () => {
  api.S.act = {
    name: 'Workout',
    curEx: 0,
    ex: [
      { id: '0025', sets: [{ done: false }] }, // Bench (idx 0)
      { id: '0043', sets: [{ done: false }] }  // Squat (idx 1)
    ]
  };

  // User on Bench Press (0) selects 0001 (Sit-up) from library
  api.addAndPairFromLibrary(0, '0001');

  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex.length, 3);
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[0].id, '0025', 'A1 is Bench at idx 0');
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[1].id, '0001', 'A2 is Sit-up inserted adjacent at idx 1');
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[2].id, '0043', 'Squat shifted to idx 2');

  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[0].ssRole, 'A');
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[1].ssRole, 'B');
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[0].ssGroup, api.S.act.ex[1].ssGroup);
});

it('unlinkSuperset safely removes superset attributes from both paired exercises', () => {
  api.unlinkSuperset(0);
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[0].ss, undefined);
  assert.equal(api.S.act.ex[1].ss, undefined);
  const ss = api.getSupersetInfo(api.S.act, 0);
  assert.equal(ss.isSuperset, false);
});

it('renderSupersetBanner renders unified SUPERSET [A1 + A2] badge and round indicator', () => {
  const act = {
